/**
 * เว็บนับสต็อก (Google Apps Script)
 * วางไฟล์นี้เป็น Code.gs ใน Apps Script ของไฟล์ Google Sheet ที่ต้องการเก็บยอดนับ
 * แล้ว Deploy เป็น Web app: Execute as = Me, Who has access = Anyone
 */
const TZ = 'Asia/Bangkok';
const WH_NAME = { PY: 'พะเยา', CM: 'เชียงใหม่', FS: 'หน้าร้าน' };
const LOG_COLS = ['id', 'เวลาบันทึก', 'วันที่', 'คลัง', 'รหัสสินค้า', 'ชื่อสินค้า', 'หน่วย', 'ต่อหน่วยเล็ก',
  'จำนวน', 'รวมหน่วยเล็ก', 'ประเภท', 'คนนับ', 'เครื่อง', 'ลบแล้ว', 'เวอร์ชัน', 'ตั้งยอดเป็น', 'หน่วยที่ตั้ง', 'เวลา(ms)'];

function doGet() {
  return HtmlService.createHtmlOutput(PAGE_HTML)
    .setTitle('นับสต็อก Chern9')
    .addMetaTag('viewport', 'width=device-width, initial-scale=1, viewport-fit=cover')
    .setXFrameOptionsMode(HtmlService.XFrameOptionsMode.ALLOWALL);
}
/* ---------- API สำหรับหน้าเว็บที่เปิดจากเว็บอื่น (เช่น GitHub Pages) ---------- */
const API_FNS = { getSession: getSession, pushEntries: pushEntries, saveSystem: saveSystem, getConfig: getConfig,
  saveLink: saveLink, uploadImage: uploadImage, exportDay: exportDay,
  addDamage: addDamage, listDamage: listDamage, deleteDamage: deleteDamage, exportDamage: exportDamage,
  saveRecon: saveRecon, getRecon: getRecon,
  addReturn: addReturn, listReturn: listReturn, deleteReturn: deleteReturn, exportBook: exportBook, fillCompany: fillCompany };
function doPost(e) {
  let out;
  try {
    const req = JSON.parse(e.postData.contents);
    if (req && Array.isArray(req.events)) return lineWebhook_(req.events);
    const fn = API_FNS[req.fn];
    if (!fn) throw new Error('unknown function');
    out = { ok: true, result: fn.apply(null, req.args || []) };
  } catch (err) {
    out = { ok: false, error: String(err && err.message || err) };
  }
  return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify(out)).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
}
function include(name) { return HtmlService.createHtmlOutputFromFile(name).getContent(); }

/* ---------- helpers ---------- */
function tab_(name, head) {
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActive();
  let sh = ss.getSheetByName(name);
  if (!sh) {
    sh = ss.insertSheet(name);
    if (head) {
      sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, head.length).setValues([head]).setFontWeight('bold');
      sh.setFrozenRows(1);
    }
  }
  return sh;
}
function logTab_(date) { return tab_('นับสต็อก_' + String(date).slice(0, 7), LOG_COLS); }
function cache_() { return CacheService.getScriptCache(); }
function sKey_(wh, date) { return 'S_' + wh + '_' + date; }
function txt_(v) { return "'" + String(v == null ? '' : v); }
function str_(v) {
  if (v instanceof Date) return Utilities.formatDate(v, TZ, 'yyyy-MM-dd');
  return String(v == null ? '' : v);
}
function withLock_(fn) {
  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  lock.waitLock(25000);
  try { return fn(); } finally { lock.releaseLock(); }
}
function checkWh_(wh) { if (!WH_NAME[wh]) throw new Error('bad warehouse'); }
function checkDate_(d) { if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(d))) throw new Error('bad date'); }

/* ---------- count entries ---------- */
function readSession_(wh, date) {
  const sh = logTab_(date);
  const n = sh.getLastRow() - 1;
  if (n < 1) return [];
  const v = sh.getRange(2, 1, n, LOG_COLS.length).getValues();
  const out = [];
  for (const r of v) {
    if (str_(r[2]) !== date || r[3] !== wh) continue;
    const e = { i: str_(r[0]), c: str_(r[4]), u: str_(r[6]), f: Number(r[7]) || 1, q: Number(r[8]) || 0,
      n: str_(r[11]), d: str_(r[12]), t: Number(r[17]) || 0 };
    if (r[10] === 'ตั้งยอด') e.k = 'set';
    if (r[13]) e.x = 1;
    if (Number(r[14])) e.m = Number(r[14]);
    if (r[15] !== '' && r[15] != null) e.tq = Number(r[15]);
    if (r[16]) e.tu = str_(r[16]);
    out.push(e);
  }
  return out;
}

function getSession(wh, date) {
  checkWh_(wh); checkDate_(date);
  const c = cache_();
  let entries;
  const hit = c.get(sKey_(wh, date));
  if (hit) entries = JSON.parse(hit);
  else {
    entries = readSession_(wh, date);
    const js = JSON.stringify(entries);
    if (js.length < 95000) c.put(sKey_(wh, date), js, 21600);
  }
  return { entries: entries, system: getSystem_(wh, date), damage: damageMap_(wh, date) };
}

function toRow_(wh, date, dev, e) {
  const q = Number(e.q) || 0, f = Number(e.f) || 1, t = Number(e.t) || Date.now();
  const type = e.x ? 'ลบ' : (e.k === 'set' ? 'ตั้งยอด' : (q < 0 ? 'ลด' : 'เพิ่ม'));
  return [txt_(e.i), new Date(t), txt_(date), wh, txt_(e.c), String(e.nm || ''), String(e.u || ''), f, q,
    Math.round(q * f * 1000) / 1000, type, String(e.n || ''), String(dev || ''), e.x ? 1 : '',
    Number(e.m || e.t) || 0, e.tq == null ? '' : Number(e.tq), String(e.tu || ''), t];
}

function pushEntries(wh, date, dev, log) {
  checkWh_(wh); checkDate_(date);
  if (!Array.isArray(log)) throw new Error('bad log');
  return withLock_(function () {
    const sh = logTab_(date);
    const n = sh.getLastRow() - 1;
    const ids = n > 0 ? sh.getRange(2, 1, n, 1).getValues() : [];
    const vers = n > 0 ? sh.getRange(2, 15, n, 1).getValues() : [];
    const idx = {};
    ids.forEach(function (r, i) { idx[str_(r[0])] = i; });
    const add = [];
    log.forEach(function (e) {
      if (!e || !e.i || !e.c) return;
      const at = idx[String(e.i)];
      if (at === undefined) { add.push(toRow_(wh, date, dev, e)); idx[String(e.i)] = -1; }
      else if (at >= 0 && (Number(e.m || e.t) || 0) > (Number(vers[at][0]) || 0)) {
        sh.getRange(at + 2, 1, 1, LOG_COLS.length).setValues([toRow_(wh, date, dev, e)]);
      }
    });
    if (add.length) { /* รายการล่าสุดอยู่บนสุด */
      add.sort(function (x, y) { return y[1].getTime() - x[1].getTime(); });
      if (sh.getLastRow() >= 2) sh.insertRowsBefore(2, add.length);
      sh.getRange(2, 1, add.length, LOG_COLS.length).setValues(add).setFontWeight('normal').setBackground(null);
    }
    cache_().remove(sKey_(wh, date));
    return { ok: true, added: add.length };
  });
}

/* ---------- system stock (ยอดระบบ) ---------- */
function getSystem_(wh, date) {
  const c = cache_(), k = 'Y_' + wh + '_' + date;
  const hit = c.get(k);
  if (hit) return JSON.parse(hit);
  const sh = tab_('ยอดระบบ', ['คีย์', 'คลัง', 'วันที่', 'ข้อมูล', 'อัปเดต']);
  const n = sh.getLastRow() - 1;
  let map = {};
  if (n > 0) {
    const v = sh.getRange(2, 1, n, 4).getValues();
    for (const r of v) if (str_(r[0]) === wh + '_' + date) { try { map = JSON.parse(r[3] || '{}'); } catch (e) {} }
  }
  const js = JSON.stringify(map);
  if (js.length < 95000) c.put(k, js, 21600);
  return map;
}
function saveSystem(wh, date, map) {
  checkWh_(wh); checkDate_(date);
  return withLock_(function () {
    const sh = tab_('ยอดระบบ', ['คีย์', 'คลัง', 'วันที่', 'ข้อมูล', 'อัปเดต']);
    const key = wh + '_' + date, js = JSON.stringify(map || {});
    const n = sh.getLastRow() - 1;
    const keys = n > 0 ? sh.getRange(2, 1, n, 1).getValues() : [];
    let row = -1;
    keys.forEach(function (r, i) { if (str_(r[0]) === key) row = i + 2; });
    const vals = [[key, WH_NAME[wh], txt_(date), js, new Date()]];
    if (row > 0) sh.getRange(row, 1, 1, 5).setValues(vals);
    else sh.getRange(sh.getLastRow() + 1, 1, 1, 5).setValues(vals);
    cache_().remove('Y_' + key);
    return true;
  });
}

/* ---------- barcode links & product photos ---------- */
function getConfig() {
  const lk = tab_('ผูกบาร์โค้ด', ['บาร์โค้ด', 'รหัสสินค้า', 'หน่วย', 'ต่อหน่วยเล็ก', 'เวลา']);
  const im = tab_('รูปสินค้าเพิ่มเอง', ['รหัสสินค้า', 'รหัสไฟล์รูป', 'เวลา']);
  const links = [], images = {};
  if (lk.getLastRow() > 1) lk.getRange(2, 1, lk.getLastRow() - 1, 4).getValues().forEach(function (r) {
    if (r[0] && r[1]) links.push({ b: str_(r[0]), c: str_(r[1]), u: str_(r[2]), f: Number(r[3]) || 1 });
  });
  if (im.getLastRow() > 1) im.getRange(2, 1, im.getLastRow() - 1, 2).getValues().forEach(function (r) {
    if (r[0] && r[1]) images[str_(r[0])] = str_(r[1]);
  });
  return { links: links, images: images };
}
function upsert_(sh, key, vals) {
  const n = sh.getLastRow() - 1;
  const keys = n > 0 ? sh.getRange(2, 1, n, 1).getValues() : [];
  let row = -1;
  keys.forEach(function (r, i) { if (str_(r[0]) === String(key)) row = i + 2; });
  if (row > 0) sh.getRange(row, 1, 1, vals.length).setValues([vals]);
  else sh.getRange(sh.getLastRow() + 1, 1, 1, vals.length).setValues([vals]);
}
function saveLink(bar, v) {
  if (!/^\d{6,14}$/.test(String(bar)) || !v || !v.c) throw new Error('bad link');
  return withLock_(function () {
    upsert_(tab_('ผูกบาร์โค้ด', ['บาร์โค้ด', 'รหัสสินค้า', 'หน่วย', 'ต่อหน่วยเล็ก', 'เวลา']), bar,
      [txt_(bar), txt_(v.c), String(v.u || ''), Number(v.f) || 1, new Date()]);
    return true;
  });
}
function folder_(name) {
  const it = DriveApp.getFoldersByName(name);
  return it.hasNext() ? it.next() : DriveApp.createFolder(name);
}
function uploadImage(code, b64) {
  if (!code || !b64 || b64.length > 8000000) throw new Error('bad image');
  const blob = Utilities.newBlob(Utilities.base64Decode(b64), 'image/jpeg', String(code).replace(/[\\/]/g, '_') + '.jpg');
  const file = folder_('รูปสินค้า (เว็บนับสต็อก)').createFile(blob);
  try { file.setSharing(DriveApp.Access.ANYONE_WITH_LINK, DriveApp.Permission.EDIT); } catch (e) {}
  withLock_(function () {
    upsert_(tab_('รูปสินค้าเพิ่มเอง', ['รหัสสินค้า', 'รหัสไฟล์รูป', 'เวลา']), code, [txt_(code), file.getId(), new Date()]);
  });
  return file.getId();
}

/* ---------- export (one spreadsheet, one tab per warehouse) ---------- */
function exportDay(date, sheets) {
  checkDate_(date);
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.create('สต็อก ' + date);
  sheets.forEach(function (s, i) {
    const rows = s.rows || [];
    const w = rows.reduce(function (m, r) { return Math.max(m, r.length); }, 1);
    const data = rows.map(function (r) { const a = r.slice(); while (a.length < w) a.push(''); return a; });
    const sh = i === 0 ? ss.getSheets()[0].setName(s.name) : ss.insertSheet(s.name);
    if (data.length) sh.getRange(1, 1, data.length, w).setValues(data);
    sh.getRange(1, 1).setFontWeight('bold');
    sh.getRange(2, 1, 1, w).setFontWeight('bold').setBackground('#e8efe9');
    sh.setFrozenRows(2);
    if (data.length > 2) sh.getRange(2, 1, data.length - 1, w).createFilter();
    sh.autoResizeColumns(1, w);
  });
  const file = DriveApp.getFileById(ss.getId());
  try { file.moveTo(folder_('รีพอตนับสต็อก')); } catch (e) {}
  try { file.setSharing(DriveApp.Access.ANYONE_WITH_LINK, DriveApp.Permission.EDIT); } catch (e) {}
  return { url: ss.getUrl(), xlsx: 'https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/' + ss.getId() + '/export?format=xlsx' };
}


/* ---------- สินค้าชำรุด / สินค้าตีกลับ (โครงเดียวกัน) ---------- */
const DMG_COLS = ['id', 'เวลาบันทึก', 'วันที่', 'คลัง', 'สาเหตุ', 'รหัสสินค้า', 'ชื่อสินค้า', 'หน่วย', 'ต่อหน่วยเล็ก',
  'จำนวน', 'รวมหน่วยเล็ก', 'หน่วยเล็กสุด', 'หมายเหตุ', 'คนบันทึก', 'รูป', 'รหัสไฟล์รูป', 'ลบแล้ว', 'ลบโดย', 'บริษัท', 'เลขคำสั่งซื้อ/แทรค'];
const KINDS = {
  dmg: { tab: 'สินค้าชำรุด', folder: 'รูปสินค้าชำรุด (เว็บนับสต็อก)', ck: 'D_', why: 'สาเหตุ' },
  ret: { tab: 'สินค้าตีกลับ', folder: 'รูปสินค้าตีกลับ (เว็บนับสต็อก)', ck: 'R_', why: 'สภาพสินค้า' }
};
function kTab_(k) {
  const K = KINDS[k]; if (!K) throw new Error('bad kind');
  const head = DMG_COLS.slice(); head[4] = K.why;
  const sh = tab_(K.tab, head);
  if (sh.getLastColumn() < DMG_COLS.length) sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, head.length).setValues([head]).setFontWeight('bold');
  return sh;
}
function kRows_(k) {
  const sh = kTab_(k), n = sh.getLastRow() - 1;
  return n > 0 ? sh.getRange(2, 1, n, DMG_COLS.length).getValues() : [];
}
function dmgObj_(r) {
  return { i: str_(r[0]), t: r[1] instanceof Date ? r[1].getTime() : 0, date: str_(r[2]), wh: str_(r[3]), why: str_(r[4]),
    c: str_(r[5]), nm: str_(r[6]), u: str_(r[7]), f: Number(r[8]) || 1, q: Number(r[9]) || 0, base: Number(r[10]) || 0,
    bu: str_(r[11]), note: str_(r[12]), n: str_(r[13]), img: str_(r[15]).split(',').filter(String), x: r[16] ? 1 : 0,
    co: str_(r[18]) || coMap_()[str_(r[5])] || '', ord: str_(r[19]) };
}
function damageMap_(wh, date) {
  const c = cache_(), key = 'D_' + wh + '_' + date, hit = c.get(key);
  if (hit) return JSON.parse(hit);
  const m = {};
  kRows_('dmg').forEach(function (r) {
    if (str_(r[3]) === wh && str_(r[2]) === date && !r[16]) m[str_(r[5])] = (m[str_(r[5])] || 0) + (Number(r[10]) || 0);
  });
  c.put(key, JSON.stringify(m), 21600);
  return m;
}
/* บริษัทของสินค้า (ฝังในไฟล์ไฟล์เดียว) — ใช้เติมให้อัตโนมัติถ้าหน้าเว็บไม่ได้ส่งมา */
function coMap_() { return (typeof CO_MAP !== 'undefined') ? CO_MAP : {}; }
function addRec_(k, rec, photos) {
  if (!rec || !rec.i || !rec.c || !(Number(rec.q) > 0)) throw new Error('bad record');
  checkWh_(rec.wh); checkDate_(rec.date);
  photos = (photos || []).slice(0, 6);
  if (!photos.length && k !== 'ret') throw new Error('ต้องมีรูปอย่างน้อย 1 รูป');
  if (kRows_(k).some(function (r) { return str_(r[0]) === String(rec.i); })) return { ok: true, dup: true };
  const fd = folder_(KINDS[k].folder), ids = [];
  photos.forEach(function (b64, n) {
    if (!b64 || b64.length > 8000000) return;
    const name = rec.date + '_' + rec.wh + '_' + String(rec.c).replace(/[\\/]/g, '_') + (rec.ord ? '_' + String(rec.ord).replace(/[\\/]/g, '_') : '') + '_' + (n + 1) + '.jpg';
    const file = fd.createFile(Utilities.newBlob(Utilities.base64Decode(b64), 'image/jpeg', name));
    try { file.setSharing(DriveApp.Access.ANYONE_WITH_LINK, DriveApp.Permission.VIEW); } catch (e) {}
    ids.push(file.getId());
  });
  const q = Number(rec.q), f = Number(rec.f) || 1;
  const row = [txt_(rec.i), new Date(Number(rec.t) || Date.now()), txt_(rec.date), rec.wh, String(rec.why || ''), txt_(rec.c),
    String(rec.nm || ''), String(rec.u || ''), f, q, Math.round(q * f * 1000) / 1000, String(rec.bu || ''), String(rec.note || ''),
    String(rec.n || ''), '', ids.join(','), '', '', String(rec.co || coMap_()[String(rec.c)] || ''), txt_(rec.ord || '')];
  withLock_(function () {
    const sh = kTab_(k), at = 2; /* รายการล่าสุดอยู่บนสุด */
    if (sh.getLastRow() >= 2) sh.insertRowBefore(2);
    sh.getRange(at, 1, 1, DMG_COLS.length).setValues([row]).setFontWeight('normal').setBackground(null);
    if (ids.length) {
      const label = ids.map(function (id, n) { return 'รูป' + (n + 1); }).join('  ');
      const rt = SpreadsheetApp.newRichTextValue().setText(label);
      let pos = 0;
      ids.forEach(function (id, n) { const t = 'รูป' + (n + 1); rt.setLinkUrl(pos, pos + t.length, 'https://drive.google.com/file/d/' + id + '/view'); pos += t.length + 2; });
      sh.getRange(at, 15).setRichTextValue(rt.build());
    }
  });
  cache_().remove(KINDS[k].ck + rec.wh + '_' + rec.date);
  if (k === 'dmg') { try { lineNotifyDmg_(rec, ids); } catch (e) {} }
  return { ok: true, img: ids };
}
function listRec_(k, from, to, wh) {
  checkDate_(from); checkDate_(to);
  const out = [];
  kRows_(k).forEach(function (r) {
    const o = dmgObj_(r);
    if (o.x || o.date < from || o.date > to || (wh && wh !== 'ALL' && o.wh !== wh)) return;
    out.push(o);
  });
  return out.sort(function (a, b) { return b.t - a.t; });
}
function delRec_(k, id, who) {
  return withLock_(function () {
    const sh = kTab_(k), v = kRows_(k);
    for (let i = 0; i < v.length; i++) if (str_(v[i][0]) === String(id)) {
      sh.getRange(i + 2, 17, 1, 2).setValues([[1, String(who || '')]]);
      cache_().remove(KINDS[k].ck + str_(v[i][3]) + '_' + str_(v[i][2]));
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  });
}
function addDamage(rec, photos) { return addRec_('dmg', rec, photos); }
function listDamage(from, to, wh) { return listRec_('dmg', from, to, wh); }
function deleteDamage(id, who) { return delRec_('dmg', id, who); }
function addReturn(rec, photos) { return addRec_('ret', rec, photos); }
function listReturn(from, to, wh) { return listRec_('ret', from, to, wh); }
function deleteReturn(id, who) { return delRec_('ret', id, who); }
/* ไฟล์ Excel หลายชีท (เช่น แยกบริษัท) — IMG:<id> = รูปตัวอย่าง, LINK:<id> = ลิงก์ "เปิดรูป" */
function exportBook(title, sheets) {
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.create(String(title || 'รีพอต'));
  (sheets || []).forEach(function (s, n) {
    const rows = s.rows || [];
    const w = rows.reduce(function (m, r) { return Math.max(m, r.length); }, 1);
    const data = rows.map(function (r) { const a = r.slice(); while (a.length < w) a.push(''); return a; });
    const imgCols = {};
    data.forEach(function (r) { r.forEach(function (v, j) {
      const m = /^(IMG|LINK):([\w-]+)$/.exec(String(v));
      if (!m) return;
      if (m[1] === 'IMG') { r[j] = '=IMAGE("https://drive.google.com/thumbnail?id=' + m[2] + '&sz=w300")'; imgCols[j] = 1; }
      else r[j] = '=HYPERLINK("https://drive.google.com/file/d/' + m[2] + '/view","เปิดรูป")';
    }); });
    const name = String(s.name || ('ชีท' + (n + 1))).replace(/[\[\]\*\?\/\\:]/g, ' ').slice(0, 90) || ('ชีท' + (n + 1));
    const sh = n === 0 ? ss.getSheets()[0].setName(name) : ss.insertSheet(name);
    if (data.length) sh.getRange(1, 1, data.length, w).setValues(data);
    sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, w).setFontWeight('bold').setBackground('#e8efe9');
    sh.setFrozenRows(1);
    if (data.length > 1) sh.getRange(1, 1, data.length, w).createFilter();
    sh.autoResizeColumns(1, w);
    if (Object.keys(imgCols).length && data.length > 1) {
      sh.setRowHeights(2, data.length - 1, 90);
      Object.keys(imgCols).forEach(function (j) { sh.setColumnWidth(Number(j) + 1, 110); });
    }
  });
  const file = DriveApp.getFileById(ss.getId());
  try { file.moveTo(folder_('รีพอตนับสต็อก')); } catch (e) {}
  try { file.setSharing(DriveApp.Access.ANYONE_WITH_LINK, DriveApp.Permission.EDIT); } catch (e) {}
  return { url: ss.getUrl(), xlsx: 'https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/' + ss.getId() + '/export?format=xlsx' };
}
function exportDamage(title, rows) { return exportBook(title, [{ name: 'สินค้าชำรุด', rows: rows }]); }

/* ---------- เทียบสต็อก (ยืนยันรีพอตรายวัน) ---------- */
const RC_COLS = ['คีย์', 'วันที่', 'คลัง', 'รหัสสินค้า', 'ชื่อสินค้า', 'หน่วยเล็กสุด', 'นับได้', 'BigSeller', 'เปิดล่วงหน้า', 'ชำรุด',
  'ยอดที่ควรมี', 'ผลต่าง', 'สถานะ', 'ยืนยันโดย', 'เวลายืนยัน'];
const RCM_COLS = ['คีย์', 'วันที่', 'คลัง', 'ยืนยันโดย', 'เวลายืนยัน', 'ข้อมูล'];
function saveRecon(wh, date, meta, rows) {
  checkWh_(wh); checkDate_(date);
  if (!Array.isArray(rows)) throw new Error('bad rows');
  const key = wh + '_' + date, now = new Date(), who = String((meta && meta.who) || '');
  return withLock_(function () {
    const sh = tab_('เทียบสต็อก', RC_COLS);
    const n = sh.getLastRow() - 1;
    let keep = n > 0 ? sh.getRange(2, 1, n, RC_COLS.length).getValues().filter(function (r) { return str_(r[0]) !== key; }) : [];
    const add = rows.map(function (r) {
      return [key, txt_(date), WH_NAME[wh], txt_(r.c), String(r.n || ''), String(r.bu || ''), num_(r.cnt), num_(r.bs), num_(r.po), num_(r.dm),
        num_(r.exp), num_(r.diff), String(r.st || ''), who, now];
    });
    const all = keep.concat(add);
    if (n > 0) sh.getRange(2, 1, n, RC_COLS.length).clearContent();
    if (all.length) sh.getRange(2, 1, all.length, RC_COLS.length).setValues(all);
    upsert_(tab_('เทียบสต็อก_ยืนยัน', RCM_COLS), key, [key, txt_(date), WH_NAME[wh], who, now, JSON.stringify(meta || {})]);
    return { ok: true, at: now.getTime() };
  });
}
function num_(v) { return v === '' || v == null || isNaN(Number(v)) ? '' : Number(v); }
function getRecon(wh, date) {
  checkWh_(wh); checkDate_(date);
  const sh = tab_('เทียบสต็อก_ยืนยัน', RCM_COLS), n = sh.getLastRow() - 1;
  if (n < 1) return null;
  const v = sh.getRange(2, 1, n, RCM_COLS.length).getValues();
  for (const r of v) if (str_(r[0]) === wh + '_' + date) {
    let m = {}; try { m = JSON.parse(r[5] || '{}'); } catch (e) {}
    return { who: str_(r[3]), at: r[4] instanceof Date ? r[4].getTime() : 0, meta: m };
  }
  return null;
}

/* เติมชื่อบริษัทย้อนหลัง ให้แถวชำรุด/ตีกลับที่ช่องบริษัทยังว่าง (map = {รหัสสินค้า: บริษัท}) */
function fillCompany(map) {
  if (!map || typeof map !== 'object') throw new Error('bad map');
  return withLock_(function () {
    const out = {};
    Object.keys(KINDS).forEach(function (k) {
      const sh = kTab_(k), n = sh.getLastRow() - 1;
      out[k] = 0;
      if (n < 1) return;
      const codes = sh.getRange(2, 6, n, 1).getValues();
      const rng = sh.getRange(2, 19, n, 1), cos = rng.getValues();
      codes.forEach(function (r, i) {
        const c = str_(r[0]);
        if (!str_(cos[i][0]) && map[c]) { cos[i][0] = String(map[c]); out[k]++; }
      });
      if (out[k]) rng.setValues(cos);
    });
    return out;
  });
}


/* ---------- แจ้งเตือน LINE OA เมื่อมีการบันทึกสินค้าชำรุด ----------
   1) ใส่ Channel access token (long-lived) ของ LINE OA ในบรรทัด LINE_TOKEN ด้านล่าง
   2) LINE Developers → Messaging API → Webhook URL = ลิงก์ /exec ของเว็บแอปนี้ → เปิด Use webhook
   3) พิมพ์ "แจ้งเตือนชำรุด 9999" หา LINE OA (หรือในกลุ่มที่เชิญ OA เข้าไป) = ลงทะเบียนรับแจ้งเตือน
      พิมพ์ "หยุดแจ้งเตือนชำรุด 9999" = ยกเลิก */
const LINE_TOKEN = '';
const WH_NAMES_ = { PY: 'พะเยา', CM: 'เชียงใหม่', FS: 'หน้าร้าน' };
const LINE_JOIN = 'แจ้งเตือนชำรุด 9999', LINE_LEAVE = 'หยุดแจ้งเตือนชำรุด 9999';
function lineTargets_() { try { return JSON.parse(PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('LINE_TO') || '[]'); } catch (e) { return []; } }
function lineApi_(path, body) {
  if (!LINE_TOKEN) return null;
  return UrlFetchApp.fetch('https://api.line.me/v2/bot/message/' + path, {
    method: 'post', contentType: 'application/json', muteHttpExceptions: true,
    headers: { Authorization: 'Bearer ' + LINE_TOKEN }, payload: JSON.stringify(body) });
}
function lineWebhook_(events) {
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  events.forEach(function (ev) {
    if (ev.type !== 'message' || !ev.message || ev.message.type !== 'text') return;
    const t = String(ev.message.text || '').trim(), src = ev.source || {};
    const id = src.groupId || src.roomId || src.userId;
    if (!id || (t !== LINE_JOIN && t !== LINE_LEAVE)) return;
    let to = lineTargets_().filter(function (x) { return x !== id; });
    if (t === LINE_JOIN) to.push(id);
    props.setProperty('LINE_TO', JSON.stringify(to));
    lineApi_('reply', { replyToken: ev.replyToken, messages: [{ type: 'text',
      text: t === LINE_JOIN ? '✅ ลงทะเบียนรับแจ้งเตือนสินค้าชำรุดแล้ว' : '⛔ ยกเลิกแจ้งเตือนสินค้าชำรุดแล้ว' }] });
  });
  return ContentService.createTextOutput('OK');
}
function lineNotifyDmg_(rec, ids) {
  const to = lineTargets_(); if (!LINE_TOKEN || !to.length) return;
  const co = rec.co || coMap_()[String(rec.c)] || '-';
  const msg = '⚠️ สินค้าชำรุด ' + (WH_NAMES_[rec.wh] || rec.wh) + '\n' +
    String(rec.nm || rec.c) + '\nรหัส ' + rec.c + '\nจำนวน ' + rec.q + ' ' + (rec.u || '') +
    '\nสาเหตุ: ' + (rec.why || '-') + '\nบริษัท: ' + co + '\nคนบันทึก: ' + (rec.n || '-') +
    (rec.date && rec.date !== Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), 'Asia/Bangkok', 'yyyy-MM-dd') ? '\nลงวันที่: ' + rec.date : '') +
    (rec.note ? '\nหมายเหตุ: ' + rec.note : '') +
    (ids.length ? '\nรูป: https://drive.google.com/file/d/' + ids[0] + '/view' : '');
  const messages = [{ type: 'text', text: msg }];
  if (ids.length) {
    const img = 'https://drive.google.com/thumbnail?id=' + ids[0] + '&sz=w1024';
    messages.push({ type: 'image', originalContentUrl: img, previewImageUrl: img });
  }
  to.forEach(function (id) {
    const r = lineApi_('push', { to: id, messages: messages });
    if (r && r.getResponseCode() >= 400 && messages.length > 1) lineApi_('push', { to: id, messages: [messages[0]] });
  });
}

if(mode!=='gas')return;\n  if(ls.get('stk_dirty_'+s)&&mine.length){pending.add(s);flush()}\n  refresh();\n}\nlet refreshing=false;\nasync function refresh(){\n  if(mode!=='gas'||refreshing)return;refreshing=true;const s=sid();\n  try{\n    const r=await gs('getSession',wh,date);if(s!==sid())return;\n rebuildDocs();render();\n    if(!pending.size&&!writing)netDown=false;\n  }catch(e){netDown=true}\n  finally{refreshing=false;syncLabel()}\n}\nasync function flush(){\n  if(writing||mode!=='gas'||!canWrite)return;writing=true;syncLabel();\n  while(pending.size){\n    const s=pending.values().next().value;pending.delete(s);\n    const [w,d]=s.split('_');const log=s===sid()?mine.slice():readLocal(s);\n    try{\n      await gs('pushEntries',w,d,deviceId,log.map(e=>Object.assign({nm:(byCode.get(e.c)||{}).n||''},e)));\n      netDown=false;backoff=1500;if(!pending.has(s))ls.set('stk_dirty_'+s,'');\n    }catch(e){\n      netDown=true;pending.add(s);writing=false;syncLabel();\n      setTimeout(flush,backoff+Math.random()*800);backoff=Math.min(backoff*2,30000);return;\n    }\n  }\n  writing=false;syncLabel();refresh();\n}\nfunction commit(){const s=sid();writeLocal(s,mine);rebuildDocs();render();if(mode==='gas'){pending.add(s);flush()}}\nwindow.addEventListener('online',()=>{backoff=1500;flush();refresh()});\ndocument.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{if(!document.hidden){backoff=1500;flush();refresh()}});\nsetInterval(()=>{if(!document.hidden&&mode==='gas')refresh()},20000);\nfunction syncBacklog(){\n  try{for(let i=0;i<localStorage.length;i++){const k=localStorage.key(i);if(k&&k.startsWith('stk_dirty_')&&localStorage.getItem(k)){const applyLink(bar,v){\n  const p=byCode.get(v.c);if(!p)return;\n  let ui=p.units.findIndex(u=>u.u===v.u&&u.f===v.f);\n  if(ui<0){p.units.push({u:v.u,f:v.f,b:bar});ui=p.units.length-1}\n  else */\nfunction allEntries(){const out=[];docs.forEach((v,id)=>{(v.log||[]).forEach(e=>{if(!e.x)out.push({...e,_doc:id,_mine:id===myDocId()})})});return out.sort((a,b)=>b.t-a.t)}\nfunction totals(){\n '+date.split('-').reverse().join('/')],head];\n  codes.forEach(({c,p})=>{\n    const base=Math.round((T.get(c)||0)*1000)/1000;\n    const parts=p?splitUnits(p,base):['','','','',base,''];\n    const });\n  return rows;\n}\n$('xlsxBtn').hidden=false;\n$('xlsxBtn').addEventListener('click',async()=>{\n  if(mode!=='gas'){toast('ส่งออกได้เมื่อเปิดผ่านลิงก์เว็บแอปเท่านั้น');return}\n  const
